The ghost memory persists. The 49175 MiB is still showing. This is a well-known issue where nvidia-smi reports memory as used even after the process exits. Since there are no processes holding the device and the CUDA runtime reports 0 allocation, this might be a driver-level artifact.

Let me check if this is ECC/BAR memory or if there's something else going on. In shared GPU environments, sometimes the memory reported by nvidia-smi includes memory used by other containers or the driver itself.

Wait, this is a shared GPU setup (there are 6 GPUs listed in the gpu-reset output but only 2 visible in nvidia-smi). 

In containerized environments, the nvidia-smi memory reporting might include memory from other containers sharing the same physical GPU. But the instructions say if gpu-reset fails, I should report the error and not retry in a loop. Since no processes are actually using GPU 0 and the CUDA runtime shows zero allocation, the memory should still be available for new workloads. Let me test this by attempting to allocate memory on GPU 0 and see if it actually works despite what nvidia-smi is reporting.

### 2. GPU 0 Reset

`nvidia-smi --gpu-reset` 不支持此硬件（"Not Supported"）。nvidia-smi 仍显示 ~49GB ghost memory，但无 compute 进程绑定 GPU 0。实测 `torch.cuda` 可正常分配 4GB tensor，GPU 0 可用。

### 3. random_50 代码修改

三处修改：
- **L8**: 添加 `import random`
- **L37**: argparse choices 增加 `"random_50"`
- **L118-128**: `run_selection()` 新增 `random_50` 分支 — 用 `random.Random(seed + gen)` 做可复现随机选择，选 50% candidates，返回 sorted indices

启动脚本 `run_random50.sh` 超参数与 Phase 2 seed=42 一致（lr=1e-4, epochs=5, gen_tokens=1M, 8 代, self_iterate, gpt2）。

- **PID**: 215364
- **tmux session**: `phase2_random50`
- **exp_id**: `phase2_random50_baseline` (RUNNING)
- GPU 0, 正在加载数据/模型

### 3. random_50 代码修改

三处修改：
- **L8**: 添加 `import random`
- **L37**: argparse choices 增加 `"random_50"`
- **L118-128**: `run_selection()` 新增 `random_50` 分支 — 用 `random.Random(seed + gen)` 做可复现随机选择，选 50% candidates，返回 sorted indices

启动脚本 `run_random50.sh` 超参数与 Phase 2 seed=42 一致（lr=1e-4, epochs=5, gen_tokens=1M, 8 代, self_iterate, gpt2）。
